## 0. The reading (stated before the numbers)

**1. What the ledger is.** Per run, cut r_c (16 and 24 r_s, R = 512 a and 768 a on r_s/a 32; the chain-end cut at site n_sites - 10 for continuity with readout 2; 8 r_s optional), slice clock and field (the heavy field; the light channels kappa 1, 2 and 3, the guard propagated unfed, with the weights 16 kappa of the md's E_inv identities and the sums over 1, 2 and 1, 2, 3 beside), the columns are the stored operators' own numbers: (a) H_in, the energy inside the cut with the continued reference, the sum over the sites inside of Re[h (C - P_ref)]; (b) H_out, the same beyond the cut, signed and unweighted per channel as 5d's exterior entries are; (c) I_E, the cut current, inward-positive (energy entering the inside), and its time integral from 40 at the slices' resolution; (d) field_work and reference_reset; (e) the cut's site index, the sites inside, the cut's identity between the two runs and the content of the sites that change sides; (f) the closure; (g) W/2g at r_c with E. The conventions (the inside rule, the light weighting of H_in_light, the current's form and sign, the index layout) are read off at the join clock against the records' own H_in, H_in_light, E_inv and chain-end currents and printed with their residuals; where no convention reproduces a record quantity the ledger is not written and nothing is substituted.

**2. The closure and the identification.** (f) = H_in(T) - H_in(40) - (the integral of (c) + the two terms of (d) + the term of (e)) is a bookkeeping column on the stored history, not a flux statement: evolution (the inside sum of Re[h (C' - C)] over a step) + work + reference + membership telescopes exactly to dH_in, so (f) equals the evolution term minus the current's integral and measures how far the trapezoid of the slice-clock currents stands from the exact evolution at the slices' resolution. The identification of (c)'s integral with the budget's evolution_exact holds at a fixed cut: at the chain end over 0 to 40 the cut moved from site 235 to 2038 and readout 2's evolution_exact (-110196) differs from the integrated current (5.199e-08) by the bare content of the swept sites, which reference_reset (+110196) carries back and (e) carries in this ledger; this readout reproduces the triple from the records before any collar term is written and estimates the swept content from the slice at 40. At the collars and at the chain end over the continuation the cut is fixed, (e) is zero, and the records' own evolution_exact, field_work and reference_reset over 40 to T are printed beside the ledger's integral, work and reference at the chain end.

**3. The matched differences.** d = C - control at the same clock on every column to the control's last clock (50 by plan #162), read as section 1 of the note reads the chain-end quantities: matched-implementation, finite-channel record differences, the gate's tolerances as pass levels for the identical command line, no tolerance stated for the collar quantities, the kept residuals as the stop rule's scales, nothing converted into an error. From 50 to 80 the member's ledger is its own, the control's absence the plan's limit and not a finding. By reorientation 4's estimate the formation's light reaches 16 r_s from about T/r_s 39 and 24 r_s from about 49: the matched window covers the estimated 16 r_s arrival throughout and the estimated 24 r_s arrival only in its last 1 r_s (16 records); the 24 r_s arrival from 50 to 80 has no control.

**4. What no column is.** No column is a global energy fraction, a radiated fraction, a flux identity, an emitted-energy bound, a feedback bound or an entropy; a term of (c) written as energy that crossed the collar is the stored current's integral with its resolution and the truncation at kappa 2 (the guard over the sourcing channels' current, 0.976 at the chain end at 40, printed at the collars beside), and only after (f) has been read at the chain end over 0 to 40 and at the collars over the continuation. delta R_L against Delta R_t is formed only at records where both runs carry a dense root; no record from 19.375 to 40 did, and a root's reappearance from 40 on is a fact of the records printed as such.

## 3. The collar ledger

Conventions: layout site_major, inside '<', the light weighting ('w', 'all'); the current column is this readout's I_C, inward-positive (the stored current's sign +1 read off at the chain end); the steps: light [0.5] r_s, heavy [1.0] r_s; the integral of (c) by the trapezoid of the slice-clock currents in T (lattice units), work, ref and memb cumulative from 40; closure = dH_in - (integral + work + ref + memb); the (g) column is W/2g at r_c, the stored W over 2 g (the spec's collar charge; the Misner-Sharp M = W/(2 g N^2) is in the json beside it and equals W/2g only where N(r_c) = 1) from the s11 npz (node spacing 4).
The heavy field is the weighted sum over the channels H1, H2, H3, H4, H5, H6, H7, H8, H9, H10 (weights 16 kappa: H1 16, H2 32, H3 48, H4 64, H5 80, H6 96, H7 112, H8 128, H9 144, H10 160; the per-channel unweighted columns are in the json and the npz under H<kappa>). The work and reference columns are the records' split (work = the inside sum of Re[(h' - h)(C' - P')], ref = -(the inside sum of Re[h (P' - P)]): engine_twofield_v3's field_work and reference_reset). ### 3.4 The chain end over the continuation: the records' own budget beside the ledger

The chain-end cut is fixed over the continuation (site [2038] at every clock), so (e) is zero and the records' evolution_exact over 40 to T is the engine's own fixed-cut term, to be matched by the ledger's integral of (c) to the resolution; the records' field_work and reference_reset over the same window beside the ledger's work and ref; the light combination light_sourcing matches the records' work and reference best (residual 0.00419).
